- **skill-portability**: This skill should be used when the user wants to check whether an agent skill is portable across providers. Common triggers include "is this skill cross-provider safe", "will my skill work in cursor", "audit skill compatibility", "check if this loads in codex", and "which providers support this skill". Spawns one agent per provider in parallel using bundled provider-doc snapshots (refreshed on cadence — never fetched at runtime) and produces a compatibility matrix plus a COMPAT.md report. Skip when authoring a new skill (use skill-creator) or rerunning baselines (use skill-eval). ([thebytefarm/ciderpress](https://github.com/thebytefarm/ciderpress/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/skill-portability) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **skill-retro**: スキル（nonstop・implement などの工程スキル）で進めた作業のあとに発生した、ユーザーとの追加のやりとり（修正指示・質問・やり直し・中断・差し戻し）を会話履歴から抽出し、1件ずつ「何がダメだったか」「どの工程で防げたか」「スキル・規約・メモリのどこを直せば次回そのラリーが消えるか」を分析して修正案を出し、承認されたものをスキルへ反映する振り返りハーネス。「振り返りして」「この開発の振り返りをしたい」「このセッションを振り返って」「ラリーを減らしたい」「なんでこんなに手戻りしたか整理して」「今回のやりとりをスキルに反映して」「nonstop の振り返り」「スキルを改善したい」などと言われたときに使う。スキルの新規作成や eval の実行そのものには使わない（skill-creator を使う）。 ([YoshimasaAsada/instructions](https://github.com/YoshimasaAsada/instructions/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-retro))

- **skill-retrospective**: Four-phase skill retrospective loop. Collects structured feedback at the end of skill sessions, groups recurring patterns, prioritizes fixes, and tracks resolutions across sessions. Use at the end of any session that used a skill to capture what went well or what could improve, or in a dedicated retro session for grouping and prioritization. Also use when the user says "let's retro", "review how the skill went", "capture feedback", "what went wrong", "log this observation", or "record a resolution".
